<script>
// ============ SOUND SYSTEM ============
const Sounds = {};
const soundFiles = ['boot','click','ding','error','minimize','maximize','close','open','alert','start','menu'];

async function loadSounds() {
  for (const name of soundFiles) {
    try {
      const key = name + '.wav';
      const url = (typeof EMBEDDED_ASSETS !== 'undefined' && EMBEDDED_ASSETS[key]) ? EMBEDDED_ASSETS[key] : `assets/sounds/${name}.wav`;
      const audio = new Audio(url);
      audio.volume = 0.6;
      Sounds[name] = audio;
    } catch(e) { console.warn('Sound load fail', name); }
  }
}
function play(name, vol = 0.7) {
  const s = Sounds[name];
  if (!s) return;
  try {
    const clone = s.cloneNode();
    clone.volume = vol;
    clone.play().catch(()=>{});
  } catch(e){}
}

// ============ WINDOW MANAGER ============
let windowZ = 10;
let openWindows = [];
let taskButtons = {};

class Win98Window {
  constructor(title, contentHTML, options = {}) {
    this.id = 'win-' + Date.now() + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2);
    this.title = title;
    this.options = { width: 420, height: 300, x: 120 + (openWindows.length % 5) * 26, y: 60 + (openWindows.length % 4) * 24, icon: '💻', ...options };
    
    this.el = document.createElement('div');
    this.el.className = 'window';
    this.el.id = this.id;
    this.el.style.left = this.options.x + 'px';
    this.el.style.top = this.options.y + 'px';
    this.el.style.width = this.options.width + 'px';
    this.el.style.height = this.options.height + 'px';
    
    this.isMaximized = false;
    this.prevRect = null;

    const icon = this.options.icon || '💻';
    this.el.innerHTML = `
      <div class="win-titlebar">
        <img class="icon" src="${this.options.iconSrc || ''}" style="display:${this.options.iconSrc ? 'block':'none'}" width="16" height="16" alt="">
        <span class="title">${title}</span>
        <div class="win-controls">
          <div class="win-btn min" title="Minimize">—</div>
          <div class="win-btn max" title="Maximize">□</div>
          <div class="win-btn close" title="Close">✕</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="win-client"></div>
      ${options.status ? `<div class="win-statusbar">${options.status}</div>` : ''}
    `;

    document.getElementById('desktop').appendChild(this.el);
    
    this.client = this.el.querySelector('.win-client');
    this.client.innerHTML = contentHTML;
    
    this.setupEvents();
    this.focus();
    
    openWindows.push(this);
    this.createTaskButton();
    
    play('open', 0.5);
  }
  
  setupEvents() {
    const titlebar = this.el.querySelector('.win-titlebar');
    
    // Drag
    let dragOffset = {x:0, y:0};
    const onMove = (e) => {
      if (this.isMaximized) return;
      const x = e.clientX - dragOffset.x;
      const y = Math.max(0, e.clientY - dragOffset.y);
      this.el.style.left = x + 'px';
      this.el.style.top = y + 'px';
    };
    const onUp = () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
      document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
    };
    titlebar.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
      if (e.target.closest('.win-btn')) return;
      this.focus();
      dragOffset = { x: e.clientX - this.el.offsetLeft, y: e.clientY - this.el.offsetTop };
      document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
      document.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
    });
    
    // Buttons
    titlebar.querySelector('.min').onclick = () => this.minimize();
    titlebar.querySelector('.max').onclick = () => this.toggleMaximize();
    titlebar.querySelector('.close').onclick = () => this.close();
    
    // Focus on any click inside
    this.el.addEventListener('mousedown', () => this.focus());
    
    // Resize (simple 4 corner/edge)
    this.makeResizable();
    
    // Double click title = maximize
    titlebar.addEventListener('dblclick', () => this.toggleMaximize());
  }
  
  makeResizable() {
    const handle = document.createElement('div');
    handle.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;right:0;bottom:0;width:14px;height:14px;cursor:nwse-resize;z-index:10;';
    this.el.appendChild(handle);
    
    let startX, startY, startW, startH;
    const move = (e) => {
      if (this.isMaximized) return;
      const nw = Math.max(240, startW + (e.clientX - startX));
      const nh = Math.max(140, startH + (e.clientY - startY));
      this.el.style.width = nw + 'px';
      this.el.style.height = nh + 'px';
    };
    const up = () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousemove', move);
      document.removeEventListener('mouseup', up);
    };
    handle.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
      e.stopImmediatePropagation();
      startX = e.clientX; startY = e.clientY;
      startW = this.el.offsetWidth; startH = this.el.offsetHeight;
      document.addEventListener('mousemove', move);
      document.addEventListener('mouseup', up);
    });
  }
  
  focus() {
    openWindows.forEach(w => w.el.classList.remove('active'));
    this.el.classList.add('active');
    this.el.style.zIndex = ++windowZ;
  }
  
  minimize() {
    this.el.style.display = 'none';
    play('minimize');
    const btn = taskButtons[this.id];
    if (btn) btn.classList.remove('active');
  }
  
  toggleMaximize() {
    if (!this.isMaximized) {
      this.prevRect = { left: this.el.style.left, top: this.el.style.top, width: this.el.style.width, height: this.el.style.height };
      this.el.classList.add('maximized');
      this.isMaximized = true;
      play('maximize');
    } else {
      this.el.classList.remove('maximized');
      if (this.prevRect) {
        Object.assign(this.el.style, this.prevRect);
      }
      this.isMaximized = false;
    }
  }
  
  close() {
    play('close', 0.55);
    const btn = taskButtons[this.id];
    if (btn) btn.remove();
    delete taskButtons[this.id];
    this.el.remove();
    openWindows = openWindows.filter(w => w.id !== this.id);
  }
  
  createTaskButton() {
    const container = document.getElementById('task-buttons');
    const btn = document.createElement('div');
    btn.className = 'task-btn active';
    btn.innerHTML = `<img src="${this.options.iconSrc || ''}" style="display:${this.options.iconSrc?'inline':'none'}" width="16" height="16"><span>${this.title}</span>`;
    btn.onclick = () => {
      if (this.el.style.display === 'none') {
        this.el.style.display = '';
        this.focus();
      } else if (this.el.classList.contains('active')) {
        this.minimize();
      } else {
        this.focus();
      }
    };
    container.appendChild(btn);
    taskButtons[this.id] = btn;
  }
  
  setTitle(t) {
    this.title = t;
    this.el.querySelector('.title').textContent = t;
    if (taskButtons[this.id]) taskButtons[this.id].querySelector('span').textContent = t;
  }
}

// ============ DESKTOP ICONS ============
const desktopIcons = [
  { id:'computer', label:'My Computer', icon:'assets/icons/computer.png', action: () => launchExplorer('computer') },
  { id:'recycle', label:'Recycle Bin', icon:'assets/icons/recycle-empty.png', action: () => launchRecycle() },
  { id:'ie', label:'Internet Explorer', icon:'assets/icons/ie.png', action: () => launchIE() },
  { id:'notepad', label:'Notepad', icon:'assets/icons/notepad.png', action: () => launchNotepad() },
  { id:'paint', label:'Paint', icon:'assets/icons/paint.png', action: () => launchPaint() },
  { id:'calc', label:'Calculator', icon:'assets/icons/calculator.png', action: () => launchCalc() },
  { id:'mines', label:'Minesweeper', icon:'assets/icons/minesweeper.png', action: () => launchMinesweeper() },
  { id:'readme', label:'Readme.txt', icon:'assets/icons/textfile.png', action: () => launchNotepad('readme') },
];

function renderDesktopIcons() {
  const cont = document.getElementById('desktop-icons');
  cont.innerHTML = '';
  desktopIcons.forEach((ic, idx) => {
    const div = document.createElement('div');
    div.className = 'desktop-icon';
    if (ic.id === 'recycle') div.dataset.id = 'recycle';
    div.innerHTML = `<img src="${ic.icon}" draggable="false"><div class="label">${ic.label}</div>`;
    
    div.addEventListener('click', () => {
      document.querySelectorAll('.desktop-icon').forEach(d => d.classList.remove('selected'));
      div.classList.add('selected');
      setTimeout(() => div.classList.remove('selected'), 1400);
    });
    div.addEventListener('dblclick', () => {
      play('click');
      ic.action();
    });
    
    // Right click selection + context
    div.addEventListener('contextmenu', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      document.querySelectorAll('.desktop-icon').forEach(d => d.classList.remove('selected'));
      div.classList.add('selected');
      showContextMenu(e.clientX, e.clientY, [
        {label: 'Open', action: ic.action},
        {label: 'Rename', action: () => alertWin98('Rename not implemented in this demo')},
        {label: 'Delete', action: () => { if(confirm('Move to Recycle Bin?')) div.remove(); }},
        {sep:true},
        {label: 'Properties', action: () => showProperties(ic.label)}
      ]);
    });
    cont.appendChild(div);
  });
}

let recycleFull = false;
function updateRecycleIcon() {
  const iconContainer = document.querySelector('.desktop-icon[data-id="recycle"]');
  if (iconContainer) {
    const img = iconContainer.querySelector('img');
    if (img) img.src = recycleFull ? 'assets/icons/recycle-full.png' : 'assets/icons/recycle-empty.png';
  }
}

// ============ START MENU ============
const startBtn = document.getElementById('start-btn');
const startMenu = document.getElementById('start-menu');

startBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
  e.stopPropagation();
  const isOpen = startMenu.classList.contains('show');
  closeAllMenus();
  if (!isOpen) {
    startMenu.classList.add('show');
    play('start');
  }
});

function closeAllMenus() {
  startMenu.classList.remove('show');
  document.getElementById('context-menu').classList.remove('show');
}

document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
  if (!e.target.closest('#start-menu') && !e.target.closest('#start-btn')) {
    closeAllMenus();
  }
});

// Start menu actions
startMenu.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
  const item = e.target.closest('.start-menu-item');
  if (!item) return;
  const action = item.dataset.action;
  const launch = item.dataset.launch;
  const file = item.dataset.file;
  
  closeAllMenus();
  
  if (launch) {
    play('click');
    if (launch === 'notepad') launchNotepad(file);
    else if (launch === 'paint') launchPaint();
    else if (launch === 'calc') launchCalc();
    else if (launch === 'explorer') launchExplorer();
    else if (launch === 'minesweeper') launchMinesweeper();
    else if (launch === 'ie') launchIE();
  } else if (action === 'shutdown') {
    showShutdownDialog();
  } else if (action === 'run') {
    showRunDialog();
  } else if (action === 'display') {
    showDisplayProperties();
  } else if (action === 'help') {
    launchNotepad('help');
  } else if (action === 'find-files') {
    alertWin98('Find: This would search the entire C:\\ drive (demo limitation).');
  } else {
    play('click');
  }
});

// ============ CONTEXT MENU ============
function showContextMenu(x, y, items) {
  const cm = document.getElementById('context-menu');
  cm.innerHTML = '';
  cm.style.left = x + 'px';
  cm.style.top = y + 'px';
  
  items.forEach(it => {
    if (it.sep) {
      const sep = document.createElement('div');
      sep.style.cssText = 'height:1px;background:#808080;margin:2px 4px;';
      cm.appendChild(sep);
      return;
    }
    const d = document.createElement('div');
    d.className = 'context-item' + (it.disabled ? ' disabled' : '');
    d.textContent = it.label;
    if (!it.disabled) d.onclick = () => { cm.classList.remove('show'); it.action(); };
    cm.appendChild(d);
  });
  cm.classList.add('show');
  
  // auto close
  setTimeout(() => {
    document.addEventListener('click', function handler(ev) {
      if (!cm.contains(ev.target)) {
        cm.classList.remove('show');
        document.removeEventListener('click', handler);
      }
    }, {once:true});
  }, 10);
}

// Desktop right click
document.getElementById('desktop').addEventListener('contextmenu', (e) => {
  if (e.target.closest('.desktop-icon') || e.target.closest('.window')) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  showContextMenu(e.clientX, e.clientY, [
    {label: 'Arrange Icons', action: () => {}},
    {label: 'Line up Icons', action: () => {}},
    {sep:true},
    {label: 'New ▶', action: () => {}},
    {sep:true},
    {label: 'Properties', action: () => showDisplayProperties()}
  ]);
});

// ============ TASKBAR CLOCK ============
function updateClock() {
  const el = document.getElementById('tray-clock');
  const now = new Date();
  let h = now.getHours();
  const m = String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0');
  const ampm = h >= 12 ? 'PM' : 'AM';
  h = h % 12 || 12;
  el.textContent = `${h}:${m} ${ampm}`;
}
setInterval(updateClock, 30000);
updateClock();

// Volume tray click - classic popup
document.getElementById('tray-volume').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
  const pop = document.createElement('div');
  pop.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;bottom:32px;right:8px;background:#c0c0c0;border:2px solid #fff;border-right-color:#404040;border-bottom-color:#404040;padding:8px 10px;z-index:10020;box-shadow:2px 2px 4px rgba(0,0,0,0.4);width:38px;';
  pop.innerHTML = `<div style="text-align:center;font-size:10px;margin-bottom:4px">Volume</div>
    <input type="range" min="0" max="100" value="65" orient="vertical" style="writing-mode:bt-lr;height:90px;width:18px" />
    <div style="text-align:center;margin-top:3px"><button class="win98-btn" style="padding:1px 6px;font-size:9px">OK</button></div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(pop);
  const slider = pop.querySelector('input');
  slider.oninput = () => { play('click', Math.max(0.1, slider.value / 140)); };
  pop.querySelector('button').onclick = () => pop.remove();
  setTimeout(() => document.addEventListener('click', function h(ev){ if(!pop.contains(ev.target)){pop.remove();document.removeEventListener('click',h);} }, {once:true}), 20);
});

// ============ APP LAUNCHERS ============
function launchNotepad(file = null) {
  let content = 'Welcome to Windows 98 Notepad!\n\nThis is a fully functional clone.\nTry the File and Edit menus.\n';
  let title = 'Untitled - Notepad';
  
  if (file === 'readme') {
    title = 'Readme.txt - Notepad';
    content = 'WINDOWS 98 HTML CLONE\n======================\n\nThank you for trying this authentic recreation!\n\nFeatures:\n- Classic UI and sounds\n- Multiple working applications\n- Minesweeper, Paint, Calculator etc.\n\nCreated with care for the 90s computing nostalgia.\n';
  } else if (file === 'story') {
    title = 'My Story.txt - Notepad';
    content = 'Once upon a time in 1998...\nI booted up my Pentium II and\nconnected to the internet with\nAOL 4.0 at 56k.\n\nThose were the days.';
  } else if (file === 'help') {
    title = 'Help - Notepad';
    content = 'WINDOWS 98 HELP (simulated)\n\nPress F1 in any app for more help.\n\nTo shut down: Click Start > Shut Down.\n\nEnjoy your time in the past!';
  }
  
  const w = new Win98Window(title, `
    <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;height:100%">
      <div style="background:#c0c0c0;border-bottom:1px solid #808080;padding:1px 2px;display:flex;gap:1px;flex-wrap:wrap">
        <span class="win98-btn" style="padding:1px 8px" onclick="notepadAction('${w.id}','new')">File</span>
        <span class="win98-btn" style="padding:1px 8px" onclick="notepadAction('${w.id}','edit')">Edit</span>
        <span class="win98-btn" style="padding:1px 8px" onclick="notepadAction('${w.id}','search')">Search</span>
        <span class="win98-btn" style="padding:1px 8px" onclick="notepadAction('${w.id}','help')">Help</span>
      </div>
      <textarea class="notepad-textarea" id="ta-${w.id}">${content}</textarea>
    </div>
  `, {width: 520, height: 340, iconSrc: 'assets/icons/notepad.png', status: 'Ln 1, Col 1'});
  
  // Make textarea nice
  setTimeout(() => {
    const ta = document.getElementById('ta-' + w.id);
    if (ta) {
      ta.focus();
      ta.selectionEnd = 0;
      ta.addEventListener('input', () => {
        w.setTitle( (ta.value.split('\n')[0].slice(0,18) || 'Untitled') + ' - Notepad' );
      });
    }
  }, 60);
  
  window['notepadAction'] = function(winId, type) {
    const ta = document.getElementById('ta-' + winId);
    if (!ta) return;
    if (type === 'new') { ta.value = ''; }
    if (type === 'edit') {
      const sel = ta.value.substring(ta.selectionStart, ta.selectionEnd) || ta.value;
      navigator.clipboard?.writeText(sel);
      play('click');
    }
    if (type === 'search') {
      const q = prompt('Find what?');
      if (q) {
        const idx = ta.value.indexOf(q);
        if (idx >= 0) { ta.focus(); ta.setSelectionRange(idx, idx + q.length); }
        else alertWin98('Cannot find "' + q + '"');
      }
    }
    if (type === 'help') {
      alertWin98('Notepad Help\n\nThis is a demo clone of classic Windows 98 Notepad.');
    }
  };
}

function launchPaint() {
  const w = new Win98Window('Untitled - Paint', `
    <div class="paint-container">
      <div class="paint-toolbar">
        <button class="win98-btn" data-tool="pencil">Pencil</button>
        <button class="win98-btn" data-tool="brush">Brush</button>
        <button class="win98-btn" data-tool="eraser">Eraser</button>
        <button class="win98-btn" data-tool="line">Line</button>
        <button class="win98-btn" data-tool="rect">Rect</button>
        <button class="win98-btn" data-tool="fill">Fill</button>
        <button class="win98-btn" onclick="clearPaint('${w.id}')">Clear</button>
        <button class="win98-btn" onclick="savePaint('${w.id}')">Save</button>
      </div>
      <canvas id="paint-${w.id}" width="520" height="340" class="paint-canvas"></canvas>
      <div class="paint-colors" id="colors-${w.id}"></div>
    </div>
  `, {width: 580, height: 440, iconSrc: 'assets/icons/paint.png'});
  
  setTimeout(() => initPaint(w.id), 80);
}

let currentPaintColor = '#000000';
function initPaint(winId) {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('paint-' + winId);
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', {alpha:true});
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
  ctx.fillRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
  ctx.lineJoin = ctx.lineCap = 'round';
  
  const colorsDiv = document.getElementById('colors-' + winId);
  const palette = ['#000000','#ffffff','#ff0000','#00ff00','#0000ff','#ffff00','#ff00ff','#00ffff','#808080','#c0c0c0','#800000','#008000','#000080','#808000','#800080','#008080'];
  palette.forEach(c => {
    const sw = document.createElement('div');
    sw.className = 'paint-color';
    sw.style.background = c;
    sw.onclick = () => { currentPaintColor = c; };
    colorsDiv.appendChild(sw);
  });
  
  let drawing = false, tool = 'pencil', lastX, lastY;
  canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', e => {
    drawing = true;
    [lastX, lastY] = [e.offsetX, e.offsetY];
    ctx.strokeStyle = currentPaintColor;
    ctx.lineWidth = (tool === 'brush') ? 5 : (tool === 'eraser' ? 12 : 1.5);
    if (tool === 'fill') { floodFill(ctx, e.offsetX, e.offsetY, currentPaintColor); drawing = false; }
  });
  canvas.addEventListener('mousemove', e => {
    if (!drawing) return;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(lastX, lastY);
    ctx.lineTo(e.offsetX, e.offsetY);
    ctx.stroke();
    [lastX, lastY] = [e.offsetX, e.offsetY];
  });
  window.addEventListener('mouseup', () => drawing = false);
  
  // toolbar
  const tb = canvas.parentElement.querySelector('.paint-toolbar');
  tb.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b => {
    b.onclick = () => {
      if (b.dataset.tool) tool = b.dataset.tool;
      tb.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(bb => bb.style.background = '');
      b.style.background = '#a0a0a0';
    };
  });
}

function floodFill(ctx, x, y, color) {
  // Simple flood fill for fun
  const w = ctx.canvas.width, h = ctx.canvas.height;
  const img = ctx.getImageData(0,0,w,h);
  const target = ctx.getImageData(x|0,y|0,1,1).data;
  const r = parseInt(color.slice(1,3),16), g=parseInt(color.slice(3,5),16), b=parseInt(color.slice(5,7),16);
  const stack = [[x|0,y|0]];
  while (stack.length) {
    const [cx,cy] = stack.pop();
    if (cx<0||cy<0||cx>=w||cy>=h) continue;
    const i = (cy*w+cx)*4;
    if (img.data[i]!==target[0]||img.data[i+1]!==target[1]||img.data[i+2]!==target[2]) continue;
    img.data[i]=r; img.data[i+1]=g; img.data[i+2]=b; img.data[i+3]=255;
    stack.push([cx+1,cy],[cx-1,cy],[cx,cy+1],[cx,cy-1]);
  }
  ctx.putImageData(img,0,0);
}
window.clearPaint = (id) => {
  const c = document.getElementById('paint-'+id);
  const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
  ctx.fillStyle='#fff'; ctx.fillRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
};
window.savePaint = (id) => {
  const c = document.getElementById('paint-'+id);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.download = 'paint.png';
  a.href = c.toDataURL();
  a.click();
  play('click');
};

function launchCalc() {
  const w = new Win98Window('Calculator', `
    <div class="calc">
      <div class="calc-display" id="calc-disp-${w.id}">0</div>
      <div class="calc-grid" id="calc-grid-${w.id}"></div>
    </div>
  `, {width: 260, height: 240, iconSrc: 'assets/icons/calculator.png'});
  
  setTimeout(() => initCalc(w.id), 50);
}

let calcState = {};
function initCalc(winId) {
  const disp = document.getElementById('calc-disp-' + winId);
  const grid = document.getElementById('calc-grid-' + winId);
  const keys = ['MC','MR','MS','M+','C','7','8','9','/','sqrt','4','5','6','*','%','1','2','3','-','=','0','+/-','.','+'];
  
  calcState[winId] = {val:'0', prev:null, op:null, mem:0};
  
  keys.forEach(k => {
    const b = document.createElement('button');
    b.className = 'calc-btn';
    b.textContent = k;
    b.onclick = () => calcPress(winId, k, disp);
    grid.appendChild(b);
  });
  
  // Keyboard support
  disp.tabIndex = 0;
  disp.onkeydown = (e) => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter') calcPress(winId, '=', disp);
    if (/[0-9.]/.test(e.key)) calcPress(winId, e.key, disp);
    if (['+','-','*','/'].includes(e.key)) calcPress(winId, e.key, disp);
  };
}

function calcPress(id, k, disp) {
  const st = calcState[id];
  if (!st) return;
  play('click', 0.4);
  
  if (k >= '0' && k <= '9' || k === '.') {
    if (st.val === '0' || st.justOp) st.val = (k === '.' ? '0.' : k);
    else st.val = st.val + k;
    st.justOp = false;
  } else if (k === '+/-') {
    st.val = String(-parseFloat(st.val));
  } else if (['+','-','*','/'].includes(k)) {
    if (st.op && !st.justOp) calcEquals(id, disp);
    st.prev = parseFloat(st.val);
    st.op = k;
    st.justOp = true;
  } else if (k === '=') {
    calcEquals(id, disp);
  } else if (k === 'C') {
    st.val='0'; st.prev=null; st.op=null;
  } else if (k === 'sqrt') {
    st.val = String(Math.sqrt(parseFloat(st.val)));
  } else if (k === '%') {
    st.val = String(parseFloat(st.val) / 100);
  } else if (k === 'MC') st.mem=0;
  else if (k === 'MR') st.val = String(st.mem);
  else if (k === 'MS') st.mem = parseFloat(st.val);
  else if (k === 'M+') st.mem += parseFloat(st.val);
  
  disp.textContent = st.val.slice(0,12);
}

function calcEquals(id, disp) {
  const st = calcState[id];
  if (!st.op || st.prev === null) return;
  let res;
  const a = st.prev, b = parseFloat(st.val);
  if (st.op==='+') res=a+b; else if (st.op==='-') res=a-b;
  else if (st.op==='*') res=a*b; else if (st.op==='/') res = b===0 ? 'ERR' : a/b;
  st.val = String(res).slice(0,12);
  st.prev = null; st.op = null; st.justOp = true;
  disp.textContent = st.val;
}

function launchMinesweeper() {
  const w = new Win98Window('Minesweeper', `
    <div class="minesweeper">
      <div class="ms-header">
        <div class="ms-count" id="ms-mines-${w.id}">010</div>
        <button id="ms-face-${w.id}" style="width:26px;height:26px;font-size:16px;background:#c0c0c0;border:2px outset #c0c0c0;cursor:default">😊</button>
        <div class="ms-timer" id="ms-timer-${w.id}">000</div>
      </div>
      <div id="ms-grid-${w.id}" class="ms-grid" style="grid-template-columns: repeat(9, 18px)"></div>
    </div>
  `, {width: 190, height: 240, iconSrc: 'assets/icons/minesweeper.png'});
  
  setTimeout(() => initMinesweeper(w.id), 60);
}

function initMinesweeper(winId) {
  const gridEl = document.getElementById('ms-grid-' + winId);
  const face = document.getElementById('ms-face-' + winId);
  const mineEl = document.getElementById('ms-mines-' + winId);
  const timeEl = document.getElementById('ms-timer-' + winId);
  
  const rows=9, cols=9, mines=10;
  let board = Array(rows).fill(0).map(()=>Array(cols).fill(0));
  let revealed = Array(rows).fill(0).map(()=>Array(cols).fill(false));
  let flagged = Array(rows).fill(0).map(()=>Array(cols).fill(false));
  let gameOver = false, started = false, timer = 0, interval = null;
  
  // place mines
  let placed = 0;
  while (placed < mines) {
    const r = Math.floor(Math.random()*rows), c = Math.floor(Math.random()*cols);
    if (board[r][c] !== -1) { board[r][c] = -1; placed++; }
  }
  // numbers
  for (let r=0;r<rows;r++) for (let c=0;c<cols;c++) {
    if (board[r][c] === -1) continue;
    let n=0;
    for (let dr=-1;dr<=1;dr++) for (let dc=-1;dc<=1;dc++) {
      const rr=r+dr, cc=c+dc; if (rr>=0&&rr<rows&&cc>=0&&cc<cols && board[rr][cc]===-1) n++;
    }
    board[r][c] = n;
  }
  
  function draw() {
    gridEl.innerHTML = '';
    for (let r=0;r<rows;r++) for (let c=0;c<cols;c++) {
      const cell = document.createElement('div');
      cell.className = 'ms-cell';
      if (revealed[r][c]) {
        cell.classList.add('revealed');
        if (board[r][c] === -1) { cell.classList.add('mine'); cell.textContent = '💣'; }
        else if (board[r][c] > 0) { cell.textContent = board[r][c]; cell.dataset.num = board[r][c]; }
      } else if (flagged[r][c]) {
        cell.classList.add('flag');
      }
      cell.onmousedown = (e) => {
        if (gameOver) return;
        if (e.button === 0) { // left
          face.textContent = '😮';
          reveal(r,c);
        } else if (e.button === 2) {
          flagged[r][c] = !flagged[r][c];
          draw();
          updateCount();
        }
      };
      gridEl.appendChild(cell);
    }
  }
  
  function updateCount() {
    const left = mines - flagged.flat().filter(Boolean).length;
    mineEl.textContent = String(left).padStart(3,'0');
  }
  
  function startTimer() {
    if (started) return;
    started = true;
    interval = setInterval(() => {
      timer = Math.min(999, timer+1);
      timeEl.textContent = String(timer).padStart(3,'0');
    }, 1000);
  }
  
  function reveal(r, c) {
    if (gameOver || revealed[r][c] || flagged[r][c]) return;
    startTimer();
    revealed[r][c] = true;
    if (board[r][c] === -1) {
      gameOver = true; face.textContent = '😵';
      clearInterval(interval);
      play('error');
      // reveal all mines
      for (let i=0;i<rows;i++) for (let j=0;j<cols;j++) if (board[i][j]===-1) revealed[i][j]=true;
      setTimeout(() => alertWin98('You hit a mine! Game over.'), 120);
    } else {
      play('click', 0.3);
      if (board[r][c] === 0) {
        for (let dr=-1;dr<=1;dr++) for (let dc=-1;dc<=1;dc++) {
          const rr=r+dr, cc=c+dc;
          if (rr>=0&&rr<rows&&cc>=0&&cc<cols) reveal(rr,cc);
        }
      }
    }
    draw();
    checkWin();
  }
  
  function checkWin() {
    let safe = 0, totalSafe = rows*cols - mines;
    for (let r=0;r<rows;r++) for (let c=0;c<cols;c++) if (revealed[r][c] && board[r][c] !== -1) safe++;
    if (safe === totalSafe) {
      gameOver = true; face.textContent = '😎';
      clearInterval(interval);
      play('ding');
      setTimeout(() => alertWin98('Congratulations! You cleared the minefield.'), 80);
      recycleFull = true; updateRecycleIcon();
    }
  }
  
  face.onmousedown = () => { face.style.borderStyle = 'inset'; };
  face.onmouseup = () => {
    face.style.borderStyle = 'outset';
    if (!gameOver) {
      // restart
      clearInterval(interval);
      initMinesweeper(winId);
    }
  };
  gridEl.oncontextmenu = e => e.preventDefault();
  
  draw();
  updateCount();
}

function launchExplorer(mode = 'normal') {
  const content = `
    <div class="explorer">
      <div class="explorer-sidebar">
        <div style="font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:4px">Folders</div>
        <div onclick="explorerNav(this,'c')" style="cursor:default">💻 C:</div>
        <div onclick="explorerNav(this,'win')" style="margin-left:12px;cursor:default">📁 Windows</div>
        <div onclick="explorerNav(this,'prog')" style="margin-left:12px;cursor:default">📁 Program Files</div>
        <div onclick="explorerNav(this,'docs')" style="margin-left:12px;cursor:default">📁 My Documents</div>
        <div style="margin-top:10px;color:#666">Desktop</div>
        <div onclick="explorerNav(this,'desktop')" style="cursor:default">🖥️ Desktop</div>
      </div>
      <div class="explorer-main" id="explorer-main-${Date.now()}">
        <div class="explorer-list" id="explorer-list"></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
  const w = new Win98Window('My Computer', content, {width: 520, height: 340, iconSrc: 'assets/icons/computer.png'});
  
  setTimeout(() => {
    const list = w.client.querySelector('.explorer-list');
    if (list) renderExplorerList(list, mode);
  }, 40);
}

window.explorerNav = function(el, folder) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.explorer-sidebar div').forEach(d => d.style.background = '');
  el.style.background = '#000080'; el.style.color = '#fff';
  const list = el.closest('.explorer').querySelector('.explorer-list');
  renderExplorerList(list, folder);
};

function renderExplorerList(container, folder) {
  container.innerHTML = '';
  let items = [];
  if (folder === 'c' || folder === 'computer') items = [
    {icon:'assets/icons/folder.png', name:'Program Files', type:'folder'},
    {icon:'assets/icons/folder.png', name:'Windows', type:'folder'},
    {icon:'assets/icons/folder.png', name:'My Documents', type:'folder'},
    {icon:'assets/icons/textfile.png', name:'AUTOEXEC.BAT', type:'file'},
  ];
  else if (folder === 'win') items = [{icon:'assets/icons/textfile.png', name:'win.ini', type:'file'}, {icon:'assets/icons/textfile.png', name:'system.ini', type:'file'}];
  else if (folder === 'prog') items = [{icon:'assets/icons/folder.png', name:'Internet Explorer', type:'folder'}, {icon:'assets/icons/folder.png', name:'Accessories', type:'folder'}];
  else items = [
    {icon:'assets/icons/notepad.png', name:'Readme.txt', type:'file', open: () => launchNotepad('readme')},
    {icon:'assets/icons/paint.png', name:'background.bmp', type:'file'},
    {icon:'assets/icons/ie.png', name:'Internet Explorer', type:'app', open: () => launchIE()},
  ];
  
  items.forEach(it => {
    const d = document.createElement('div');
    d.className = 'explorer-item';
    d.innerHTML = `<img src="${it.icon}"><div>${it.name}</div>`;
    d.ondblclick = () => {
      if (it.type === 'folder') {
        // simple nav
        const main = d.closest('.explorer-main');
        renderExplorerList(main.querySelector('.explorer-list'), 'docs');
      } else if (it.open) it.open();
      else if (it.type === 'file') launchNotepad('readme');
    };
    container.appendChild(d);
  });
}

function launchIE() {
  const w = new Win98Window('Microsoft Internet Explorer', `
    <div style="height:100%;display:flex;flex-direction:column">
      <div class="ie-chrome">
        <div class="ie-toolbar">
          <button class="win98-btn" onclick="ieNav('${w.id}','back')">◀ Back</button>
          <button class="win98-btn" onclick="ieNav('${w.id}','forward')">Forward ▶</button>
          <button class="win98-btn" onclick="ieNav('${w.id}','stop')">Stop</button>
          <button class="win98-btn" onclick="ieNav('${w.id}','home')">Home</button>
        </div>
        <div class="ie-address">
          <span>Address:</span>
          <input id="ie-addr-${w.id}" value="http://www.microsoft.com/windows98/" onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter') ieGo('${w.id}')">
          <button class="win98-btn" onclick="ieGo('${w.id}')" style="padding:1px 10px">Go</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="ie-content" id="ie-content-${w.id}"></div>
      <div class="ie-status">Done</div>
    </div>
  `, {width: 620, height: 420, iconSrc: 'assets/icons/ie.png'});
  
  setTimeout(() => {
    ieNav(w.id, 'home');
  }, 80);
}

window.ieNav = function(id, act) {
  const content = document.getElementById('ie-content-' + id);
  if (!content) return;
  play('click', 0.4);
  if (act === 'home' || act === 'stop') {
    content.innerHTML = `
      <h1>Welcome to the Internet!</h1>
      <p style="font-size:13px">You are connected through <strong>AOL</strong> at 56k.</p>
      <marquee behavior="alternate">🌐 The World Wide Web awaits you in 1998!</marquee>
      <p><a href="#" onclick="ieGoToPage('${id}','ms')">Visit microsoft.com</a></p>
      <p><a href="#" onclick="ieGoToPage('${id}','web')">Explore the Web</a></p>
      <p><a href="#" onclick="ieGoToPage('${id}','fun')">Fun Sites of 1998</a></p>
      <hr>
      <small>Microsoft Internet Explorer 4.0 (compatible; Windows 98)</small>
    `;
  } else if (act === 'back') {
    content.innerHTML = '<p>Previous page not cached in this demo.</p>';
  }
};

window.ieGo = function(id) {
  const input = document.getElementById('ie-addr-' + id);
  const content = document.getElementById('ie-content-' + id);
  if (!content || !input) return;
  content.innerHTML = `<h2 style="color:#008000">Connecting to ${input.value}...</h2><p>Downloading 28% complete...</p>`;
  setTimeout(() => {
    content.innerHTML = `<h1>Page Loaded</h1><p>This is a simulated 1998 web page for <strong>${input.value}</strong>.</p><p><em>Geocities would be proud.</em></p>`;
  }, 900);
};

window.ieGoToPage = function(id, page) {
  const content = document.getElementById('ie-content-' + id);
  if (page === 'ms') content.innerHTML = `<h1>Microsoft Windows 98</h1><p>Where do you want to go today?</p><p><a onclick="ieNav('${id}','home')">Back home</a></p>`;
  if (page === 'web') content.innerHTML = `<h1>The Web Ring</h1><marquee>Best viewed in Netscape Navigator or IE 4</marquee><p>Under construction since 1997 🛠️</p>`;
  if (page === 'fun') content.innerHTML = `<h1>Top 1998 Sites</h1><ul><li>Hotmail</li><li>Yahoo!</li><li>AltaVista</li><li>eBay</li></ul>`;
};

// ============ DIALOGS ============
function alertWin98(msg) {
  const d = document.createElement('div');
  d.className = 'dialog';
  d.style.left = (window.innerWidth/2 - 150) + 'px';
  d.style.top = (window.innerHeight/2 - 90) + 'px';
  d.innerHTML = `
    <div class="dialog-title">Windows</div>
    <div class="dialog-body">${msg.replace(/\n/g,'<br>')}</div>
    <div class="dialog-buttons">
      <button class="win98-btn" onclick="this.closest('.dialog').remove(); play('click')">OK</button>
    </div>
  `;
  document.getElementById('desktop').appendChild(d);
  play('ding');
}

function showShutdownDialog() {
  const d = document.createElement('div');
  d.className = 'dialog';
  d.style.left = '50%'; d.style.top = '40%'; d.style.transform = 'translate(-50%,-50%)';
  d.innerHTML = `
    <div class="dialog-title">Shut Down Windows</div>
    <div class="dialog-body">
      Are you sure you want to:<br><br>
      <label><input type="radio" name="sd" value="shutdown" checked> Shut down the computer?</label><br>
      <label><input type="radio" name="sd" value="restart"> Restart the computer?</label><br>
      <label><input type="radio" name="sd" value="standby"> Stand by?</label>
    </div>
    <div class="dialog-buttons">
      <button class="win98-btn" id="sd-ok">OK</button>
      <button class="win98-btn" onclick="this.closest('.dialog').remove()">Cancel</button>
    </div>
  `;
  document.body.appendChild(d);
  play('alert');
  
  d.querySelector('#sd-ok').onclick = () => {
    d.remove();
    const choice = d.querySelector('input:checked').value;
    doShutdown(choice);
  };
}

function doShutdown(type) {
  const overlay = document.createElement('div');
  overlay.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;inset:0;background:#000;color:#c0c0c0;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;z-index:999999;font-family:monospace;flex-direction:column';
  if (type === 'shutdown') {
    overlay.innerHTML = `<div style="font-size:13px">It is now safe to turn off your computer.</div><div style="margin-top:30px;font-size:10px;opacity:0.6">Windows 98</div>`;
  } else if (type === 'restart') {
    overlay.innerHTML = `<div>Restarting...</div>`;
    setTimeout(() => location.reload(), 1600);
  } else {
    overlay.innerHTML = `<div>Standby mode (demo ends here)</div>`;
    setTimeout(() => overlay.remove(), 1800);
  }
  document.body.appendChild(overlay);
  play('close');
}

function showRunDialog() {
  const d = document.createElement('div');
  d.className = 'dialog';
  d.style.left = '30%'; d.style.top = '35%';
  d.innerHTML = `
    <div class="dialog-title">Run</div>
    <div class="dialog-body">
      Type the name of a program, folder, or document, and Windows will open it for you.<br><br>
      <input id="run-input" style="width:100%;padding:3px" value="notepad">
    </div>
    <div class="dialog-buttons">
      <button class="win98-btn" id="run-ok">OK</button>
      <button class="win98-btn" onclick="this.closest('.dialog').remove()">Cancel</button>
    </div>
  `;
  document.body.appendChild(d);
  const inp = d.querySelector('#run-input');
  inp.focus(); inp.select();
  d.querySelector('#run-ok').onclick = () => {
    const val = inp.value.trim().toLowerCase();
    d.remove();
    if (val.includes('notepad') || val === 'notepad') launchNotepad();
    else if (val.includes('calc')) launchCalc();
    else if (val.includes('paint')) launchPaint();
    else if (val.includes('mine')) launchMinesweeper();
    else if (val.includes('explorer') || val.includes('computer')) launchExplorer();
    else if (val.includes('ie') || val.includes('internet')) launchIE();
    else alertWin98('Cannot find the file "' + val + '".');
  };
}

function showDisplayProperties() {
  const d = document.createElement('div');
  d.className = 'dialog';
  d.style.left = '25%'; d.style.top = '20%';
  d.innerHTML = `
    <div class="dialog-title">Display Properties</div>
    <div class="dialog-body" style="min-height:160px">
      <strong>Background</strong><br>
      <select id="wp-select">
        <option value="default">Windows 98 Default</option>
        <option value="teal">Solid Teal</option>
        <option value="gray">Solid Gray</option>
      </select>
      <br><br>
      <em>Note: In this demo only a few options are functional.</em>
    </div>
    <div class="dialog-buttons">
      <button class="win98-btn" id="dp-ok">OK</button>
      <button class="win98-btn" onclick="this.closest('.dialog').remove()">Cancel</button>
      <button class="win98-btn" onclick="applyWallpaper(this)">Apply</button>
    </div>
  `;
  document.body.appendChild(d);
  d.querySelector('#dp-ok').onclick = () => { applyWallpaper(d.querySelector('#dp-ok')); d.remove(); };
}

function showProperties(name) {
  alertWin98(`${name}\n\nType: System Object\nLocation: C:\\Windows\\Desktop\n\n(Properties dialog is simulated)`);
}

// ============ RECYCLE BIN ============
function launchRecycle() {
  const w = new Win98Window('Recycle Bin', `
    <div style="padding:12px;height:100%;overflow:auto">
      <p><strong>Recycle Bin</strong> contains files you deleted.</p>
      <div id="recycle-list" style="margin-top:10px;font-size:11px"></div>
      <div style="margin-top:16px">
        <button class="win98-btn" onclick="emptyRecycle()">Empty Recycle Bin</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `, {width: 380, height: 260, iconSrc: recycleFull ? 'assets/icons/recycle-full.png' : 'assets/icons/recycle-empty.png'});
  
  setTimeout(() => {
    const list = w.el.querySelector('#recycle-list');
    if (list) list.innerHTML = recycleFull ? 
      '• old_notes.txt &nbsp;&nbsp; 1.2 KB<br>• background.bmp &nbsp;&nbsp; 48 KB' : 
      '<em>The Recycle Bin is empty.</em>';
  }, 50);
}

window.emptyRecycle = function() {
  recycleFull = false;
  updateRecycleIcon();
  alertWin98('Recycle Bin emptied.');
  // close any open recycle windows
  document.querySelectorAll('.window').forEach(el => {
    if (el.textContent.includes('Recycle Bin')) el.remove();
  });
};

// ============ BOOT SEQUENCE ============
function bootSequence() {
  const boot = document.getElementById('boot-screen');
  const bar = document.getElementById('boot-bar');
  let pct = 0;
  
  const iv = setInterval(() => {
    pct += Math.random() * 22 + 7;
    if (pct > 100) pct = 100;
    bar.style.width = pct + '%';
    
    if (pct >= 100) {
      clearInterval(iv);
      setTimeout(() => {
        boot.style.transition = 'opacity 280ms ease';
        boot.style.opacity = '0';
        setTimeout(() => {
          boot.style.display = 'none';
          // play boot sound now that user has "interacted" with page
          play('boot', 0.85);
          // small welcome
          setTimeout(() => {
            const hint = document.createElement('div');
            hint.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;bottom:48px;left:12px;background:rgba(0,0,0,0.4);color:#ddd;padding:2px 6px;font-size:10px;pointer-events:none';
            hint.textContent = 'Right-click desktop for more options';
            document.body.appendChild(hint);
            setTimeout(() => hint.remove(), 4200);
          }, 1400);
        }, 280);
      }, 280);
    }
  }, 110);
}

// ============ INIT ============
async function initWin98() {
  await loadSounds();
  
  renderDesktopIcons();
  updateClock();
  
  // Make desktop icons selectable by clicking blank area
  document.getElementById('desktop').addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
    if (e.target.id === 'desktop' || e.target.id === 'desktop-icons') {
      document.querySelectorAll('.desktop-icon').forEach(d => d.classList.remove('selected'));
    }
  });
  
  // Keyboard fun
  document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
    if (e.key === 'F1') {
      e.preventDefault();
      launchNotepad('help');
    }
    if (e.metaKey && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'r') {
      e.preventDefault();
      showRunDialog();
    }
  });
  
  // Start the boot
  bootSequence();
  
  // Easter egg: Konami-ish
  let kon = '';
  document.addEventListener('keypress', (e) => {
    kon += e.key;
    if (kon.length > 6) kon = kon.slice(-6);
    if (kon.includes('win98') || kon.includes('98')) {
      kon = '';
      const d = document.createElement('div');
      d.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;top:30%;left:50%;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);background:#ffff00;color:#000;padding:6px 14px;border:2px solid #000;z-index:9999';
      d.textContent = '★ You found the secret 1998 mode! ★';
      document.body.appendChild(d);
      setTimeout(() => d.remove(), 1900);
    }
  });
  
  // Make sure sounds can play after first click anywhere
  document.body.addEventListener('click', () => {
    // unlock audio
    Object.values(Sounds).forEach(s => { try { s.play().then(()=>s.pause()).catch(()=>{}); } catch(e){} });
  }, {once:true});
  
  console.log('%c[Windows 98] Clone initialized successfully.', 'color:#0a0');
}

// Launch immediately
initWin98();
</script>
</body>
</html>
